15848042 C~DESCRIPTION OF THE DATABASE FORMAT~
15848096 C~THE DATA FILE CONTAINS SEVERAL SECTIONS.~EACH BEGINS WITH A LINE CON
15848171 C~A NUMBER IDENTIFYING THE SECTION, AND ENDS WITH A LINE CONTAINING "-1
15848252 C~SECTION 1: LONG FORM D
15848285 ESCRIPTIONS.~EACH LINE CONTAINS A LOCATION NUM
15848336 CyA TAB, AND A LINE OF TEXT.~THE SET OF (NECESSARILY ADJACENT) LI
15848405 CyWHOSE NUMBERS ARE X FORM THE LONG DESCRIPTION OF LOCATION X. 
15848471 C~SECTION 2: SHORT FORM DESCRIPTIONS.~SAME FORMAT AS LONG FORM.~NOT A
15848543 CyPLACES HAVE SHORT DESCRIPTIONS.
15848579 C~SECTION 3: TRAVEL TABLE.~EACH LINE CONTAINS A LOCATION NUMBER (X), A
15848654 CyLOCATION NUMBER (Y), AND A LIST OF MOTION NUMBERS (SEE SECTION 4
15848723 CyEACH MOTION REPRESENTS A VERB WHICH WILL GO TO Y IF CURRENTLY AT
15848792 CyY, IN TURN, IS INTERPRETED AS FOLLOWS.~LET M=Y/1000, N=Y MOD
15848858 CqIF N<=300yIT IS THE LOCATION TO GO TO.
15848903 CqIF 300<N<=500}N-300 IS USED IN A COMPUTED G
15848957 OTO TO~
15848969 CYA SECTION OF SPECIAL CODE.
15849002 CqIF N>500xMESSAGE N-500 FROM SECTION 6 IS PRINTED,
15849056 CYAND HE STAYS WHEREVER HE IS. 
15849092 CyMEANWHILE, M SPECIFIES THE CONDITIONS ON THE MOTION.
15849149 CqIF M=0vIT'S UNCONDITIONAL.
15849182 CqIF 0<M<100zIT IS DONE WITH M% PROBABILITY.
15849230 CqIF M=100xUNCONDITIONAL, BUT FORBIDDEN TO DWARVES.
15849284 CqIF 100<M<=200}HE MUST BE CARRYING OBJECT M-100. 
15849338 CqIF 200<M<=300}MUST BE CARRYING OR IN SAME ROOM AS M-20
15849398 CqIF 300<M<=400}PROP(M MOD 100) MUST *NOT* BE 0.~
15849452 CqIF 400<M<=500}PROP(M MOD 100) MUST *NOT* BE 1.~
15849506 CqIF 500<M<=600}PROP(M MOD 100) MUST *NOT* BE 2, ETC.
15849563 CyIF THE CONDITION (IF ANY) IS NOT MET, THEN THE NEXT *DI
15849643 FFERENT* 
15849655 Cy"DESTINATION" VALUE IS USED (UNLESS IT FAILS TO MEET *ITS* CONDI
15849724 CyIN WHICH CASE THE NEXT IS FOUND, ETC.).~TYPICALLY, THE NEXT DES
15849793 CyBE FOR ONE OF THE SAME VERBS, SO THAT ITS ONLY USE IS AS THE ALT
15849862 CyDESTINATION FOR THOSE VERBS.~FOR INSTANCE:
15849910 Cq15z110022~29z31z34z35z23
15849940 Cq15z14z29~
15849955 CyTHIS SAYS THAT, FROM LOC 15, ANY OF THE VERBS 29, 31, ETC., WILL
15850024 CyHIM TO 22 IF HE'S CARRYING OBJECT 10, AND OTHERWISE WILL GO TO 1
15850093 Cq11z303008~49~
15850111 Cq11z9y50~
15850126 CyTHIS SAYS THAT, FROM 11, 49 TAKES HIM TO 8 UNLESS PROP(3)=0, IN 
15850195 CyCASE HE GOES TO 9.~VERB 50 TAKES HIM TO 9 REGARDLESS OF PROP(3)
15850264 C~SECTION 4: VOCABULARY.~EACH LINE CONTAIN
15850315 S A NUMBER (N), A TAB, AND A
15850348 CyFIVE-LETTER WORD.~CALL M=N/1000.~IF M=0, THEN THE WORD IS A MO
15850417 CyVERB FOR USE IN TRAVELLING (SEE SECTION 3).~ELSE, IF M=1, THE W
15850486 CyAN OBJECT.~ELSE, IF M=2, THE WORD IS AN ACTION VERB (SUCH AS "C
15850555 CyOR "ATTACK").~ELSE, IF M=3, THE WORD IS A SPECIAL CASE VERB (SU
15850624 Cy"DIG") AND N MOD 1000 IS AN INDEX INTO SECTION 6.~OBJECTS FRO~
15850693 Cy(CURRENTLY, ANYWAY) 79 ARE CONSIDERED TREASURES (FOR PIRATE, CLO
15850762 C~SECTION 5: OBJECT DESCRIPTIONS.~EACH LINE CONTAINS A NUMBER (N), A T
15850837 CyAND A MESSAGE.~IF N IS FROM 1 TO 100, THE MESSAGE IS THE "INVEN
15850906 CyMESSAGE FOR OBJECT N.~OTHERWISE, N SHOULD BE 000, 100, 200, ETC
15850987 HE MESSAGE SHOULD BE THE DESCRIPTION OF THE PRECEDING OBJECT WH
15851053 CyPROP VALUE IS N/100.~THE N/100 IS USED ONLY TO DISTINGUISH MULT
15851122 CyMESSAGES FROM MULTI-LINE MESSAGES; THE PROP INFO ACTUALLY REQUIR
15851191 CyMESSAGES FOR AN OBJECT TO BE PRESENT AND CONSECUTIVE.~PROPERTIE
15851260 CyPRODUCE NO MESSAGE SHOULD BE GIVEN THE MESSAGE ">$<".~
15851320 C~SECTION 6: ARBITRARY MESSAGES.~SAME FORMAT AS SECTIONS 1, 2, AND 5, 
15851395 CyTHE NUMBERS BEAR NO RELATION TO ANYTHING (EXCEPT FOR SPECIAL VER
15851464 CyIN SECTION 4).~
15851485 C~SECTION 7: OBJECT LOCATIONS.~EACH LINE CONTAINS AN OBJECT NUMBER AND
15851560 CyINITIAL LOCATION (ZERO (OR OMITTED) IF NONE).~IF THE OBJECT IS 
15851629 CyIMMOVABLE, THE LOCA
15851659 TION IS FOLLOWED BY A "-1".~IF IT HAS TWO LO
15851707 Cy(E.G. THE GRATE) THE FIRST LOCATION IS FOLLOWED WITH THE SECOND,
15851776 CyTHE OBJECT IS ASSUMED TO BE IMMOVABLE.~
15851821 C~SECTION 8: ACTION DEFAULTS.~EACH LINE CONTAINS AN "ACTION-VERB" NUMB
15851896 CyTHE INDEX (IN SECTION 6) OF THE DEFAULT MESSAGE FOR THE VERB.
15851962 C~SECTION 9: LIQUID ASSETS, ETC.~EACH LINE CONTAINS A NUMBER (N) AND U
15852037 CyLOCATION NUMBERS.~BIT N (WHERE 0 IS THE UNITS BIT) IS SET IN CO
15852106 CyFOR EACH LOC GIVEN.~THE COND BITS CURRENTLY ASSIGNED ARE:
15852169 Cq0yLIGHT 
15852184 Cq1yIF BIT 2 IS ON: ON FOR OIL, OFF FOR WATER 
15852235 Cq2yLIQUID ASSET, SEE BIT 1 
15852268 Cq3yPIRATE DOESN'T GO HERE UNLESS FOLLOWING PLAYER~
15852345 CyOTHER BITS ARE USED TO INDICATE AREAS OF INTEREST TO "HINT" ROUT

15907756 C~CAVE CLOSING AND SCORING
15907798 C~THESE SECTIONS HANDLE THE CLOSING OF THE CAVE.~THE CAVE CLOSES "CLOC
15907873 C~TURNS AFTER THE LAST TREASURE HAS BEEN LOCATED (INCLUDING THE PIRATE'
15907948 C~CHEST, WHICH MAY OF COURSE NEVER SHOW UP).~NOTE THAT THE TREASURES N
15908023 C~HAVE BEEN TAKEN YET, JUST LOCATED.~HENCE CLOCK1 MUST BE LARGE ENOUGH
15908098 C~OUT OF THE CAVE (IT ONLY TICKS WHILE INSIDE THE CAVE).~WHEN IT HITS 
15908173 C~WE BRANCH TO 10000 TO START CLOSING THE CAVE, AND THEN SIT BACK AND W
15908248 C~HIM TO TRY TO GET OUT.~IF HE DOESN'T WITHIN CLOCK2 TURNS, WE CLOSE T
15908323 C~CAVE; IF HE DOES TRY, WE ASSUME HE PANICS, AND GIVE HIM A FEW ADDIT
15908407 C~TURNS TO GET FRANTIC BEFORE WE CLOSE.~WHEN CLOCK2 HITS ZERO, WE BRAN
15908482 C~11000 TO TRANSPORT HIM INTO THE FINAL PUZZLE.~NOTE THAT THE PUZZLE D
15908557 C~UPON ALL SORTS OF RANDOM THINGS.~FOR INSTANCE, THERE MUST BE NO WATE
15908632 C~OIL, SINCE THERE ARE BEANSTALKS WHICH WE DON'T WANT TO BE ABLE TO WAT
15908707 C~SINCE THE CODE CAN'T HANDLE IT.~ALSO, WE CAN HAVE NO KEYS, SINCE THE
15908782 C~GRATE (HAVING MOVED THE FIXED OBJECT!) THERE SEPARATING HIM FROM ALL 
15908857 C~TREASURES.~MOST OF THESE PROBLEMS ARISE FROM THE USE OF NEGATIVE PRO
15908932 C~NUMBERS TO SUPPRESS THE OBJECT DESCRIPTIONS UNTIL HE'S ACTUALLY MOVED
15909007 C~OBJECTS. 
15909028 C~WHEN THE FIRST WARNING COMES, WE L
15909087 OCK THE GRATE, DESTROY THE BRIDGE,

15916273 C~INTERNAL/EXTERNAL CHARACTER SET CONVERSION UTILITIES (CODE1, CODE2,~
15916348 C}DCODE1, CVLTUC, CVSTB) 
15916390 zINTEGER*6 FUNCTION CODE1(WORDS)~
15916432 C~CONVERT EXTERNAL CHARACTERS TO INTERNAL FORMAT (5 CHARS/INTEGER*
15916522 C~THE FIRST FIVE CHARACTERS OF WORDS ARE CONVERTED TO THEIR INTERNAL
15916594 C~REPRESENTATION (SIXBIT).~IF A CHARACTER HAS NO REPRESENTATION, IT IS
15916669 C~REPLACED BY A PERIOD.
15916702 C~DEFINITION OF CONSTANTS:
15916732 CyNWORDS = NUMBER OF INTEGER*6 VARIABLES NEEDED TO HOLD FIVE CHARS
15916801 CyNCHARS = NUMBER OF CHARACTERS STORED IN AN INTEGER*6 VARIABLE
15916867 CyCHRSIZ = NUMBER OF BITS REQUIRED TO REPRESENT A CHARACTER 
15916930 CyCHRMSK = NUMBER TO AND WITH AN INTEGER*6 TO OBTAIN HIGH-ORDER
15916996 CpCHARACTER.
15917017 C~(SEE CONVERSION GUIDE)~
15917053 zIMPLICIT INTEGER*6(A-Z) 
15917083 zINTEGER*3 CHRMSK,WORDS,WORD,CHAR,CHRSET
15917128 zDIMENSION WORDS(2)
15917152 zDIMENSION CHRSET(
15917208 zDATA NWORDS/2/,NCHARS/3/,CHRSIZ/8/,CHRMSK/'77600000/~
15917271 zDATA CHRSET/1H ,1H!,1H",1H#,1H$,1H%,1H&,1H', 
15917322 {1t1H(,1H),1H*,1H+,1H,,1H-,1H.,1H/, 
15917361 {2t1H0,1H1,1H2,1H3,1H4,1H5,1H6,1H7, 
15917400 {3t1H8,1H9,1H:,1H;,1H<,1H=,1H>,1H?, 
15917439 {4t1H@,1HA,1HB,1HC,1HD,1HE,1HF,1HG, 
15917478 {5t1HH,1HI,1HJ,1HK,1HL,1HM,1HN,1HO, 
15917517 {6t1HP,1HQ,1HR,1HS,1HT,1HU,1HV,1HW, 
15917556 {7t1HX,1HY,1HZ,1H[,'\',1H],1H_,1H_/ 
15917607 zRESULT=0 
15917622 zCOUNT=0~
15917640 zDO 10 I=1,NWORDS~
15917661 wWORD=WORDS(I)~
15917685 wDO 5 J=1,NCHARS
15917706 tCOUNT=COUNT+1~
15917724 tIF(COUNT.GT.5)GOTO 20
15917751 CuCHAR=AND(WORD,CHRMSK)
15917778 CuWORD=SHIFT(WORD,CHRSIZ) 
15917808 zCHAR=WORD.AND.CHRMSK 
15917835 zWORD=WORD.SHIFT.8 
15917868 tDO 1 CHRIDX=1,64~
15917889 CrIF(CHAR.EQ.AND(CHRSET(CHRIDX),CHRMSK))GOTO 2 
15917940 pIF(CHAR.EQ.(CHRSET(CHRIDX).AND.CHRMSK)) GOTO 2 
15917991 |1yCONTINUE 
15918006 tCHRIDX=15
15918021 |2yRESULT=SHIFT(RESULT,6D)+CHRIDX-1 
15918060 |5|CONTINUE 
15918075 }10 CONTINUE 
15918099 }20 CODE1=RESULT
15918120 zRETURN
15918159 zINTEGER*6 FUNCTION CODE2(CHARS)~
15918201 C~CONVERT EXTERNAL CHARACTERS TO INTERNAL FORMAT (5 CHARS/INTEGER*6).~
15918282 C~CHARS CONTAINS FIVE CHARACTERS IN A1 FORMAT.~THEY ARE CONVERTED TO~
15918354 C~THEIR INTERNAL REPRESENTATION (SIXBIT).~IF A CHARACTER~
15918414 C~HAS NO REPRESENTATION, IT IS REPLACED BY A PERIOD.

15918537 ER*6(A-Z)

15918660 {1t1H(,1H),1H*,1H+,1H,,1H-,1H.,1H/, 
15918699 {2t1H0,1H1,1H2,1H3,1H4,1H5,1H6,1H7, 
15918738 {3t1H8,1H9,1H:,1H;,1H<,1H=,1H>,1H?,

15919209 BROUTINE DCODE1(VALUE,RESULT)~
15919248 C~CONVERT INTERNAL CHARACTERS TO EXTERNAL FORMAT. 
15919308 C~VALUE CONTAINS FIVE CHARACTERS IN SIXBIT.~THEY ARE CONVERTED~
15919374 C~TO A1 FORMAT AND PLACED INTO RESULT(1) TO RESULT(5).~
15919440 C~(SEE CONVERSION GUIDE)~

15919614 {1t1H(,1H),1H*,1H+,1H,,1H-,1H.,1H/, 
15919653 {2t1H0,1H1,1H2,1H3,1H4,1H5,1H6,1H7, 
15919692 {3t1H8,1H9,1H:,1H;,1H<,1H=,1H>,1H?, 
15919731 {4t1H@,1HA,1HB,1HC,1HD,1HE,1HF,1HG, 
15919770 {5t1HH,1HI,1HJ,1HK,1HL,1HM,1HN,1HO, 
15919809 {6t1HP,1HQ,1HR,1HS,1HT,1HU,1HV,1HW, 
15919848 {7t1HX,1HY,1HZ,1H[,'\',1
15919895 H],1H_,1H_/ 
15919922 zVALCPY=VALUE
15919946 zDO 10 I=1,5 
15919964 wII=6-I
15919976 wCHRIDX=LOWSIX(VALCPY)+1 
15920006 wVALCPY=VALCPY/64~
15920027 wRESULT(II)=CHRSET(CHRIDX)~
15920057 }10 CONTINUE 
15920081 zRETURN
15920120 zSUBROUTINE CVLTUC(TEXT,LTEXT) 
15920162 C~CONVERT LOWER CASE CHARACTERS TO UPPER CASE. 
15920219 C (SEE CONVERSION GUIDE)
15920252 zIMPLICIT INTEGER*6(A-Z) 
15920282 zDIMENSION TEXT(70)
15920312 zDIMENSION UPPER(26),LOWER(26) 
15920348 zDATA UPPER/1HA,1HB,1HC,1HD,1HE,1HF,1HG,1HH,1HI,1HJ,1HK,1HL,1HM,
15920417 {1u1HN,1HO,1HP,1HQ,1HR,1HS,1HT,1HU,1HV,1HW,1HX,1HY,1HZ/,~
15920477 {2{LOWER/1Ha,1Hb,1Hc,1Hd,1He,1Hf,1Hg,1Hh,1Hi,1Hj,1Hk,1Hl,1Hm,
15920543 {3u1Hn,1Ho,1Hp,
15920567 1Hq,1Hr,1Hs,1Ht,1Hu,1Hv,1Hw,1Hx,1Hy,1Hz/
15920624 zDO 10 I=1,LTEXT
15920645 wCHR=TEXT(I) 
15920663 wDO 5 J=1,26 
15920681 sIF(CHR.NE.LOWER(J))GOTO 5 
15920711 sTEXT(I)=UPPER(J) 
15920732 sGOTO 10 
15920744 |5|CONTINUE 
15920759 }10 CONTINUE 
15920783 zRETURN
15920822 zINTEGER*6 FUNCTION CVSTB(WORD1,WORD1X) 
15920873 C~INTERNAL CHARACTER SET TO INTEGER*6 VALUE (BINARY NUMBER).~
15920945 C~WORD1 AND WORD1X CONTAIN UP TO TEN NON-BLANK CHARACTERS IN SIXBIT 
15921017 C~REPRESENTING AN INTEGER*6 VALUE.~IF A NON-DIGIT IS ENCOUNTERED IN TH
15921092 C~STRING, IT IS IGNORED.~
15921128 C~(SEE CONVERSION GUIDE)~
15921164 zIMPLICIT INTEGER*6(A-Z) 
15921194 zLOGICAL NEGATE 
15921215 zDIMENSION TEXT
15921254 zDIMENSION DIGITS(10) 
15921281 zDATA DIGITS/1H0,1H1,1H2,1H3,1H4,1H5,1H6,1H7,1H8,1H9/~
15921338 zDATA BLANK,MINUS,PLUS/' ','-','+'/~
15921389 zCALL DCODE1(WORD1,TEXT(1)) 
15921422 zCALL DCODE1(WORD1X,TEXT(6))
15921455 zRESULT=0 
15921470 zNEGATE=.FALSE. 
15921506 zIF(TEXT(1).NE.MINUS)GOTO 1 
15921539 zNEGATE=.TRUE.~
15921566 zGOTO 2
15921584 |1 IF(TEXT(1).NE.PLUS)GOTO 2~
15921617 zNEGATE=.FALSE. 
15921653 |2 DO 10 I=S,10
15921671 wIF(TEXT(I).EQ.BLANK)GOTO 20
15921704 wDO 5 J=1,10 
15921722 tIF(TEXT(I).EQ.DIGITS(J))GOTO 6
15921758 |5|CONTINUE 
15921773 wGOTO 10~
15921785 |6|RESULT=10*RESULT+J-1 
15921812 }10 CONTINUE 
15921836 }20 IF(NEGATE)RESULT=-RESULT
15921869 zCVSTB=RESULT
15921887 zRETURN
15921935 zSUBROUTINE SPEAK(N)~

15922040 C~UNLESS BLKLIN IS FALSE.

15922280 zDATA BLANK/' '/
15922313 zIF(N.EQ.0)RETURN~
15922334 zIF(LINES(N+1).EQ.CODE1('>$<~'))RETURN 
15922382 zIF(BLKLIN)WRITE(TTYO,1) 
15922412 |1 FORMAT(1X)~
15922451 }10 NWORDS=IABS(LINES(K))-K-1~
15922484 zIF(NWORDS.EQ.0)GOTO 40~
15922517 zNCHARS=5*NWORDS
15922538 zDO 15 I=1,NWORDS~

15922597 wTIDX=5*(I-1)+1 
15922618 wCALL DCODE1(LINES(LIDX),TEXT(TIDX)) 
15922660 }15 CONTINUE 
15922678 zWRITE(TTYO,20)(TEXT(I),I=1,NCHARS)~
15922717 }20 FORMAT(1X,70A1)
15922747 }30 K=IABS(LINES(K))~
15922771 zIF(LINES(K).GE.0)GOTO 10
15922801 zRETURN
15922819 }40 WRITE(TTYO,1)~
15922840 zGOTO 30~

15923701 zSUBROUTINE MSPEAK(I) 
15923734 C~PRINT THE I-TH "MAGIC" MESSAGE (SECTION 12 OF DATABASE).

15923974 zSUBROUTINE GETIN(WORD1,WORD1X,WORD2,WORD2X,NULLOK) 
15924037 C~GET A COMMAND FROM THE ADVENTURER.~
15924085 C~WORD1 IS SET TO THE FIRST FIVE CHARACTERS OF THE FIRST WORD AND
15924154 C~WORD1X IS SET TO THE SECOND FIVE.~WORD2 AND WORD2X ARE USED IN
15924223 C~AN ANALAGOUS FASHION FOR THE SECOND WORD.~IF THERE IS NO SECOND~
15924292 C~WORD, WORD2 IS SET TO ZERO.
15924325 C~IF NULLOK IS .TRUE. AND A BLANK LINE IS SUPPLIED, WORD1 IS SET TO ZER
15924400 C~OTHERWISE, THE USER MUST TYPE A NON-BLANK RESPONSE.
15924463 zIMPLICIT INTEGER*6(A-Z) 
15924493 zINTEGER*3 TTYI,TTYO,DBFI
15924523 zLOGICAL NULLOK,BLKLIN,NULL,LGWORD
15924568 zCOMMON /IOSCOM/ TTYI,TTYO,BLKLIN,DB
15924625 zDIMENSION LINE(70),CHARS(5)
15924658 zDATA BLANK/' '/
15924691 zWORD1=0~
15924703 zWORD1X=0 
15924718 zWORD2=0~
15924730 zWORD2X=0 
15924751 zIF(BLKLIN)WRITE(TTYO,1) 
15924781 |1 FORMAT(1X)~
15924805 |2 READ(TTYI,3)LINE~
15924829 |3 FORMAT(70A1)
15924859 C~CHECK FOR A NULL RESPONSE~
15924898 zNULL = .TRUE.~
15924916 zDO 4 I =1,70
15924934 wIF(LINE(I).NE.BLANK)NULL=.FALSE. 
15924973 |4 CONTINUE 
15924994 zIF(NULL.AND..NOT.NULLOK)GOTO 2
15925030 zIF(NULL.AND.NULLOK)RETURN~
15925066 zCALL CVLTUC(LINE,70D)
15925105 C~PROCESS THE FIRST WORD~
15925141 zDO 10 WDST=1,70
15925162 wIF(LINE(WDST).NE.BLANK)GOTO 11
15925198 }10 CONTINUE 
15925216 zCALL BUG(29D)~
15925240 }11 RETPNT=1 
15925258 zGOTO 1000
15925299  WORD1=CODE2(CHARS)
15925329 zIF(.NOT.LGWORD)GOTO 20~
15925356 zRETPNT=2 
15925371 zGOTO 1000
15925386 }15 WORD1X=CODE2(CHARS)~
15925419 zIF(.NOT.LGWORD)GOTO 20~
15925446 zDO 16 WDST=WDST,70
15925470 wIF(LINE(WDST).EQ.BLANK)GOTO 20
15925506 }16 CONTINUE 
15925524 zRETURN
15925548 C~PROCESS SECOND WORD (IF ANY)~
15925590 }20 IF(WDST.GT.70)RETURN 
15925620 zDO 21 WDST=WDST,70
15925644 wIF(LINE(WDST).NE.BLANK)GOTO 25
15925680 }21 CONTINUE 
15925698 zRETURN
15925716 }25 RETPNT=3 
15925734 zGOTO 1000
15925749 }30 WORD2=CODE2(CHARS)
15925782 zIF(.NOT.LGWORD)RETURN
15925809 zRETPNT=4 
15925824 zGOTO 1000
15925839 }35 WORD2X=CODE2(CHARS)~
15925866 zRETURN
15925890 C~'INTERNAL SUBROUTINE' TO GET FIVE CHARACTERS (OR LESS) FROM CURRENT~
15925974 C~WORD AND INDICATE IF WORD IS OVER FIVE CHARACTER LONG.
15926040  1000 DO 1001 I=1,5~
15926064  1001 CHARS(I)=BLANK 
15926094 zWDEND=MIN2(WDST+4,70D)~
15926121 zDO 1002 I=WDST,WDEND 
15926148 wIF(LINE(I).EQ.BLANK)GOTO 1010 
15926184 wJ=I-WDST+1~
15926199 wCHARS(J)=LINE(I)~
15926220  1002 CONTINUE 
15926244 zWDST=WDST+5 
15926262 zIF(LINE(WDST).NE.BLANK)LGWORD=.TRUE.
15926304 zIF(WDST.GT.70)LGWORD=.FALSE.~
15926337 zGOTO 1099
15926358  1010 WDST=I
15926373 zLGWORD=.FALSE. 
15926400  1099 GOTO(12,15,30,35),RETPNT

15927717 zSUBROUTINE A5TOA1(A,B,C,INSBLK,CHARS,LENG)
15927771 C~A AND B CONTAIN A 1- TO 10-CHARACTER WORD IN SIXBIT, C CONTAINS ANOTH
15927846 C~WORD AND/OR PUNCTUATION.~THEY ARE UNPACKED TO ONE CHARACTER PER WORD
15927921 C~ARRAY CHARS, WITH EXACTLY ONE BLANK BETWEEN B AND C IF 
15928001 INSBLK IS .TRU
15928019 C~(OTHERWISE, NO BLANK IS INSERTED).~
15928061 C~THE INDEX OF THE LAST NON-BLANK CHAR IN CHARS IS RETURNED IN LENG.
15928139 zIMPLICIT INTEGER*6(A-Z) 
15928169 zLOGICAL INSBLK 
15928190 zDIMENSION CHARS(20)~
15928214 zDATA BLANK/' '/
15928247 zCALL DCODE1(A,CHARS(1)) 
15928277 zCALL DCODE1(B,CHARS(6)) 
15928313 zDO 1 I=1,10 
15928331 wII=11-I~
15928343 wIF(CHARS(II).NE.BLANK)GOTO 2~
15928376 |1 CONTINUE 
15928391 zII=0~
15928406 |2 IF(.NOT.INSBLK)GOTO 3
15928433 zII=II+1~
15928445 zCHARS(II)=BLANK
15928472 |3 II=II+1~
15928487 zCALL DCODE1(C,CHARS(II))
15928523 zDO 4 I=1,5~
15928538 wLENG=II+5-I 
15928556 wIF(CHARS(LENG).NE.BLANK)RETURN
15928592 |4 CONTINUE 
15928613 zLENG=II-1
15928628 zIF(INSBLK)LENG=LENG-1
15928655 zRETURN
15928703 C~DATA STRUCTURE ROUTINES (VOCAB, DSTROY, JUGGLE, MOVE, PUT, CARRY, DRO
15928790 zINTEGER*6 FUNCTION VOCAB(ID,INIT)
15928835 C~LOOK UP ID IN THE VOCABULARY (ATAB) AND RETURN ITS "DEFINITION" (KTAB
15928910 C~-1 IF NOT FOUND.~IF INIT IS POSITIVE, THIS IS AN INITIALIZATION CALL
15928985 C~UP A KEYWORD VARIABLE, AND NOT FINDING IT CONSTITUTES A BUG.~IT ALSO
15929060 C~THAT ONLY KTAB VALUES WHICH TAKEN OVER 1000 EQUAL INIT MAY BE CONSIDE
15929135 C~(THUS "STEPS", WHICH IS A MOTION VERB AS WELL AS AN OBJECT, MAY BE LO
15929210 C~AS AN OBJECT.)~AND IT ALSO MEANS THE KTAB VALUE IS TAKEN MOD 1000.~
15929288 zIMPLICIT INTEGER*6(A-Z) 
15929318 zCOMMON /VOCCOM/ K
15929345 TAB,ATAB,TABSIZ 
15929366 zDIMENSION KTAB(300),ATAB(300) 
15929408 zHASH=SCRMBL(ID)
15929429 zDO 1 I=1,TABSIZ
15929450 zIF(KTAB(I).EQ.-1)GOTO 2 
15929480 zIF(INIT.GE.0.AND.KTAB(I)/1000.NE.INIT)GOTO 1 
15929531 zIF(ATAB(I).EQ.HASH)GOTO 3~
15929561 |1 CONTINUE 
15929576 zCALL BUG(21D)~
15929600 |2 VOCAB=-1 
15929615 zIF(INIT.LT.0)RETURN~
15929639 zCALL BUG(5D)
15929663 |3 V=KTAB(I)
15929678 zVOCAB=KTAB(I)~
15929696 zIF(INIT.GE.0)VOCAB=MOD2(V,1000D) 
15929735 zRETURN

15938594 zSUBROUTINE HOURS~
15938621 C~ANNOUNCE THE CURRENT HOURS WHEN THE CAVE IS OPEN FOR ADVENTURING.~TH
15938696 C~IS STORED IN WKDAY, WKEND, AND HOLID, WHERE BIT SHIFT(1,N) IS ON IFF 
15938771 C~HOUR FROM N:0
15938809 0 TO N:59 IS "PRIME TIME" (CAVE CLOSED).~WKDAY IS FOR~
15938866 C~WEEKDAYS, WKEND FOR WEEKENDS, HOLID FOR HOLIDAYS.~NEXT HOLIDAY IS FR
15938941 C~HBEGIN TO HEND.

15938998 zINTEGER*3 TTYI,TTYO,DBFI
15939028 zLOGICAL BLKLIN 
15939049 zDIMENSION HNAME(20)~
15939073 zCOMMON /WIZCOM/ WKDAY,WKEND,HOLID,HBEGIN,HEND,HNAME,~
15939130 {1SHORT,MAGIC,MAGNM,LATNCY,SAVED,SAVET,SETUP
15939178 zCOMMON /IOSCOM/ TTYI,TTYO,BLKLIN,DBFI~
15939232 zWRITE(TTYO,1)~
15939250 |1 FORMAT(' ')

15939301 zCALL HOURSX(WKEND,2D)

15942630 zSUBROUTINE MOTD(ALTER)~
15942663 C~HANDLES MESSAGE OF THE DAY.~IF ALTER IS TRUE, READ A NEW MESSAGE FRO
15942738 C~WIZARD.~ELSE PRINT THE CURRENT ONE.~MESSAGE IS INITIALLY NULL.~
15942813 zIMPLICIT INTEGER*6(A-Z) 
15942855 TEGER*3 TTYI,TTYO,DBFI
15942882 zLOGICAL ALTER,BLKLIN 
15942915 zCOMMON /IOSCOM/ TTYI,TTYO,BLKLIN,DBFI~
15942957 zCOMMON /MTDCOM/ MTDTXT~
15942990 zDIMENSION MTDTXT(100),TEXT(70)
15943026 zDATA BLANK/' '/,PERIOD/'.'/
15943071 zIF(ALTER)GOTO 50~
15943113 }10 IF(MTDTXT(K).LT.0)RETURN
15943146 zNWORDS=MTDTXT(K)-K-1 
15943173 zIF(NWORDS.EQ.0)GOTO 40~

15943227 zDO 15 I=1,NWORDS~
15943248 wMIDX=K+I 
15943263 wTIDX=5*(I-1)+1 
15943284 wCALL DCODE1(MTDTXT(MIDX),TEXT(TIDX))
15943326 }15 CONTINUE 
15943344 zWRITE(TTYO,20)(TEXT(I),I=1,NCHARS)~
15943383 }20 FORMAT(1X,70A1)
15943413 }30 K=MTDTXT(K) 
15943434 zGOTO 10~
15943452 }40 WRITE(TTYO,45) 
15943476 }45 FORMAT(1X)~
15943494 zGOTO 30~
15943527 }50 M=1
15943539 zCALL MSPEAK(23D)~
15943566 }55 READ(TTYI,56)TEXT,K~
15943593 }56 FORMAT(70A1,A1)
15943617 zIF(K.EQ.BLANK)GOTO 60
15943644 zCALL MSPEAK(24D)~
15943665 zGOTO 55~
15943683 }60 DO 62 I=1,70
15943704 wK=71-I
15943716 wIF(TEXT(K).NE.BLANK)GOTO 65
15943749 }62 CONTINUE 
15943776 zGOTO 70~
15943794 }65 IF((K.EQ.1).AND.(TEXT(1).EQ.PERIOD))GOTO 90~
15943851 zCALL CVLTUC(TEXT,K)~
15943875 zK=(K+4)/5
15943890 zDO 66 I=1,K 
15943908 wK1=M+I
15943920 wK2=5*(I-1)+1
15943938 wMTDTXT(K1)=CODE2(TEXT(K2)) 
15943971 }66 CONTINUE 
15943995 }70 MTDTXT(M)=M+K+1
15944019 zM=M+K+1~
15944031 zIF(M+14.LT.100)GOTO 55~
15944058 zCALL MSPEAK(25D)~
15944085 }90 MTDTXT(M)=-1
15944106 zRETURN

15945785 zSUBROUTINE BUG(NUM)~
15945809 zIMPLICIT INTEGER*6(A-Z) 
15945839 zINTEGER*3 TTYI,TTYO,DBFI
15945869 zLOGICAL BLKLIN 
15945890 zCOMMON /IOSCOM/ TTYI,TTYO,BLKLIN,DBFI~
15945938 C~THE FOLLOWING CONDITIONS ARE CURRENTLY CONSIDERED FATAL BUGS.~NUMBER
15946013 C~ARE DETECTED WHILE READING THE DATABASE; THE OTHERS OCCUR AT "RUN TIM
15946088 Cy0yMESSAGE LINE > 70 CHARACTERS 
15946124 Cy1yNULL LINE IN MESSAGE
15946151 Cy2yTOO MANY WORDS OF MESSAGES
15946184 Cy3yTOO MANY TRAVEL OPTIONS
15946214 Cy4yTO
15946229 O MANY VOCABULARY WORDS 
15946256 Cy5yREQUIRED VOCABULARY WORD NOT FOUND 
15946298 Cy6yTOO MANY RTEXT OR MTEXT MESSAGES
15946337 Cy7yTOO MANY HINTS
15946358 Cy8yLOCATION HAS COND BIT BEING SET TWICE 
15946403 Cy9yINVALID SECTION NUMBER IN DATABASE 
15946445 Cz20ySPECIAL TRAVEL (500>L>300) EXCEEDS GOTO LIST
15946499 Cz21yRAN OFF END OF VOCABULARY TABLE 
15946541 Cz22yVOCABULARY TYPE (N/1000) NOT BETWEEN 0 AND 3
15946595 Cz23yINTRANSITIVE ACTION VERB EXCEEDS GOTO LIST~
15946646 Cz24yTRANSITIVE ACTION VERB EXCEEDS GOTO LIST 
15946697 Cz25yCONDITIONAL TRAVEL ENTRY WITH NO ALTERNATIVE
15946751 Cz26yLOCATION HAS NO TRAVEL ENTRIES~
15946790 Cz27yHINT NUMBER EXCEEDS GOTO LIST
15946829 Cz28yINVALID MONTH RETURNED BY DATE FUNCTION~
15946877 Cz29yINTERNAL E
15946915 RROR IN GETIN (POSSIBLE FORTRAN BUG) 
15946963 zWRITE(TTYO,1)NUM~
15946984 |1 FORMAT (' FATAL ERROR, SEE SOURCE CODE FOR INTERPRETATION.',/~
15947053 {1' PROBABLY CAUSE: ERRONEOUS INFO IN DATABASE.',/
15947107 {2' ERROR CODE =',I2/) 
15947134 zCALL ABORT~

15949330 zSUBROUTINE DATIME(D,T)~
15949363 C~RETURN THE CURRENT DATE AND TIME.
15949408 C~D IS SET TO THE NUMBER OF DAYS SINCE 07/01/77 (A SATURDAY)~
15949474 C~T IS SET TO THE NUMBER OF MINUTES PAST MIDNIGHT.
15949540 zIMPLICIT INTEGER*6(A-Z) 
15949570 zINTEGER*3 DAY,YEAR,TIME
15949623 zDIMENSION J(2) 
15949650 zCALL JDATE(J)~
15949668 zYEAR=J(1).SHIFT.-12~
15949692 zDAY=J(1).AND.'7777
15949716 zTIME=J(2)
15949731 zD=DAY+365*(YEAR-1977)+(YEAR-1977)/4+181
15949776 zT=TIME/600~
15949791 zRETURN
15949803 zEND$~

16868164 zSUBROUTINE TAGDIF(ITA,MA,ITE,ME,IS,N,IERR)
16868218 C~UNTERPROGRAMM ZUR BERECHNUNG DER TAGE, DIE ZWEI DATEN 
16868278 C~INNERHALB EINES JAHRES VONEINANDER TRENNEN.~
